REPRODUCTION SOURCE — PERSONAL NON-PRODUCTION RESEARCH

The following code blocks preserve the runnable research source inside this prompt so the requested delivery remains two documents. They are research tools, not the trading application. Copy each named block into the indicated local folder. Use Python 3.12, numpy 2.3.5 and pandas 2.2.3. Read all experiment limits above before interpreting any result. The source archive manifests below preserve hashes; do not replace a missing archive with a convenient substitute. Downloaded or changed data must match the recorded experiment provenance.

Trading_Research_Batch_01/research_backtest.py

```python
"""Personal, non-production research. No broker or order-submission capability.
Fixed-rule bar tests; not an exact replication of any published paper.
Run: python research_backtest.py --download --run --self-test
"""
from pathlib import Path
import argparse, concurrent.futures, datetime as dt, hashlib, io, json, sys, urllib.request, zipfile
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
SYMBOLS=['BTCUSDT','ETHUSDT','SOLUSDT','DOGEUSDT','SHIBUSDT','PEPEUSDT']
STRATEGIES=['ST002_EMA20_50','ST003_DONCHIAN20_10','ST024_UTC_VWAP_REVERSION','ST021_UTC_ORB15']
COSTS={'frictionless':(0,0),'base_assumption':(10,2),'stress_10bp_slippage':(10,10),'stress_50bp_slippage':(10,50),'stress_100bp_slippage':(10,100)}
PERIODS={'2025_full':('2025-01-01','2026-01-01'),'2025_H1':('2025-01-01','2025-07-01'),'2025_H2':('2025-07-01','2026-01-01')}
CONFIG={'symbols':SYMBOLS,'source_interval':'5m','intervals_minutes':[5,15],'year':2025,'strategies':STRATEGIES,'costs_bps_per_side':COSTS,'initial_equity_usdt':10000,'allocation_fraction':0.10,'prior_bar_quote_volume_cap':0.001,'minimum_order_notional_usdt':10,'max_hold_bars':12,'close_evaluated_stop_atr_multiple':2,'earliest_entry_utc':'00:30','last_entry_utc':'23:30','mandatory_exit_open_utc':'23:45','warmup_bars':100,'selection':'six named surviving CEX assets selected before results; not a complete universe','scope':'long-only spot, one position per independent run, no leverage, no optimization','periods':PERIODS}

def download_one(item):
 symbol,month=item
 name=f'{symbol}-5m-2025-{month:02d}.zip'
 url=f'https://data.binance.vision/data/spot/monthly/klines/{symbol}/5m/{name}'
 path=ROOT/'data'/name; check_path=path.with_suffix('.zip.CHECKSUM')
 if not path.exists():
  payload=urllib.request.urlopen(url,timeout=40).read();path.write_bytes(payload)
 if not check_path.exists():check_path.write_bytes(urllib.request.urlopen(url+'.CHECKSUM',timeout=40).read())
 expected=check_path.read_text().split()[0];actual=hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
 if actual!=expected:raise ValueError('Checksum mismatch '+name)
 return {'symbol':symbol,'month':f'2025-{month:02d}','file':name,'url':url,'sha256':actual,'bytes':path.stat().st_size,'checksum_verified':True}

def download():
 (ROOT/'data').mkdir(exist_ok=True);manifest=[];errors=[]
 with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as pool:
  fs={pool.submit(download_one,(s,m)):(s,m) for s in SYMBOLS for m in range(1,13)}
  for f in concurrent.futures.as_completed(fs):
   try:manifest.append(f.result())
   except Exception as exc:errors.append({'item':fs[f],'error':str(exc)})
   if (len(manifest)+len(errors))%12==0:print('Archives checked',len(manifest),'errors',len(errors),flush=True)
 out={'retrieved_at_utc':dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).isoformat(),'provider':'Binance Vision','terms_url':'https://github.com/binance/binance-public-data/blob/master/TERMS_AND_CONDITIONS.md','license':'CC BY-NC-SA 4.0 as stated in inspected terms; personal non-production research only','archives':sorted(manifest,key=lambda x:x['file']),'errors':errors}
 (ROOT/'data_manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(out,indent=2))
 if errors:raise RuntimeError('Incomplete download; inspect data_manifest.json; no silent substitution.')

def load_data(symbol):
 names=['time','open','high','low','close','volume','close_time','quote_volume','trades','taker_base','taker_quote','ignore']
 frames=[]
 for month in range(1,13):
  path=ROOT/'data'/f'{symbol}-5m-2025-{month:02d}.zip'
  expected=path.with_suffix('.zip.CHECKSUM').read_text().split()[0]
  if hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()!=expected:raise ValueError('Changed archive '+path.name)
  with zipfile.ZipFile(path) as z:
   files=[n for n in z.namelist() if n.endswith('.csv')]
   if len(files)!=1:raise ValueError('Unexpected archive contents')
   frames.append(pd.read_csv(z.open(files[0]),header=None,names=names))
 df=pd.concat(frames,ignore_index=True)
 df['time']=pd.to_datetime(df['time'],unit='us',utc=True)
 if df.time.duplicated().any():raise ValueError('Duplicate timestamps '+symbol)
 df=df.set_index('time').sort_index()
 expected=pd.date_range('2025-01-01','2026-01-01',freq='5min',inclusive='left',tz='UTC')
 if not df.index.equals(expected):raise ValueError('Missing or extra bars '+symbol)
 for col in ['open','high','low','close','volume','quote_volume']:
  if not np.isfinite(df[col]).all():raise ValueError('Nonfinite data')
 if (df[['open','high','low','close']]<=0).any().any():raise ValueError('Nonpositive price')
 if (df.high<df[['open','close','low']].max(axis=1)).any() or (df.low>df[['open','close','high']].min(axis=1)).any():raise ValueError('Impossible OHLC')
 if (df[['volume','quote_volume']]<0).any().any():raise ValueError('Negative volume')
 return df

def bars(df,minutes):
 if minutes==5:return df.copy()
 g=df.resample(f'{minutes}min',label='left',closed='left')
 result=g.agg({'open':'first','high':'max','low':'min','close':'last','volume':'sum','quote_volume':'sum','trades':'sum'})
 if not (g.size()==minutes//5).all():raise ValueError('Partial aggregate bar')
 return result

def features(df):
 f=df.copy();c=f.close
 f['fast']=c.ewm(span=20,adjust=False,min_periods=50).mean();f['slow']=c.ewm(span=50,adjust=False,min_periods=50).mean()
 f['upper']=f.high.shift(1).rolling(20).max();f['lower']=f.low.shift(1).rolling(10).min()
 prev=c.shift(1)
 tr=pd.concat([f.high-f.low,(f.high-prev).abs(),(f.low-prev).abs()],axis=1).max(axis=1)
 f['atr']=tr.rolling(14).mean()
 day=f.index.floor('D');typical=(f.high+f.low+f.close)/3
 f['vwap']=(typical*f.volume).groupby(day).cumsum()/f.volume.groupby(day).cumsum().replace(0,np.nan)
 f['price_std']=c.rolling(20).std(ddof=0)
 mins=f.index.hour*60+f.index.minute
 # Only first 15 minutes enter the opening range. ffill occurs AFTER those bars;
 # unlike a full-day group transform this cannot reveal later opening bars early.
 first_high=f.high.where(mins<15).groupby(day).cummax()
 f['orb_high']=first_high.groupby(day).ffill()
 return f

def run_one(f,strategy,fee_bp,slip_bp,start,end,record=False):
 idx=f.index;inside=(idx>=pd.Timestamp(start,tz='UTC'))&(idx<pd.Timestamp(end,tz='UTC'));locs=np.flatnonzero(inside)
 opens=f.open.to_numpy();closes=f.close.to_numpy();qv=f.quote_volume.to_numpy();atr=f.atr.to_numpy()
 fast=f.fast.to_numpy();slow=f.slow.to_numpy();upper=f.upper.to_numpy();lower=f.lower.to_numpy();vw=f.vwap.to_numpy();std=f.price_std.to_numpy();orb=f.orb_high.to_numpy()
 mins=idx.hour.to_numpy()*60+idx.minute.to_numpy();days=idx.strftime('%Y-%m-%d').to_numpy()
 fee=fee_bp/10000;slip=slip_bp/10000;cash=10000.;qty=0.;entry_i=-1;entry_cost=0.;stop=np.nan;last_orb_day=None
 equity=[];trades=[];daily={};cost_total=0.;exposed=0
 for i in locs:
  j=i-1;exited=False
  if qty:
   signal_exit=(closes[j]<fast[j]) if strategy.startswith('ST002') else (closes[j]<lower[j]) if strategy.startswith('ST003') else (closes[j]>=vw[j]) if strategy.startswith('ST024') else (closes[j]<orb[j])
   reason='session' if mins[i]>=1425 else 'time' if i-entry_i>=12 else 'stop_close' if closes[j]<=stop else 'signal' if signal_exit else None
   if reason:
    price=opens[i]*(1-slip);value=qty*price;exitfee=value*fee;proceeds=value-exitfee
    cost_total+=exitfee+qty*opens[i]*slip;cash+=proceeds
    trades.append({'entry_time':str(idx[entry_i]),'exit_time':str(idx[i]),'entry_price':entry_price,'exit_price':price,'quantity':qty,'entry_cost':entry_cost,'net_pnl':proceeds-entry_cost,'return_on_entry_cost':(proceeds-entry_cost)/entry_cost,'exit_reason':reason,'bars_held':i-entry_i})
    qty=0.;exited=True
  if not qty and not exited and i>=100 and mins[i]>=30 and mins[i]<=1410 and np.isfinite(atr[j]) and atr[j]>0 and days[j]==days[i]:
   sig=(fast[j]>slow[j] and closes[j]>fast[j]) if strategy.startswith('ST002') else (closes[j]>upper[j]) if strategy.startswith('ST003') else (closes[j]<vw[j]-2*std[j]) if strategy.startswith('ST024') else (closes[j]>orb[j] and mins[j]>=15 and last_orb_day!=days[i])
   if sig:
    budget=min(0.10*cash,0.001*qv[j]);entry_price=opens[i]*(1+slip)
    if budget>=10:
     qty=budget/(entry_price*(1+fee));entry_cost=budget;cash-=budget;entry_i=i;stop=entry_price-2*atr[j]
     cost_total+=qty*entry_price*fee+qty*opens[i]*slip
     if strategy.startswith('ST021'):last_orb_day=days[i]
  exposed+=int(qty>0);equity.append(cash+qty*closes[i]);daily[days[i]]=equity[-1]
 if qty:raise AssertionError('Position remained open at sample end; no fabricated liquidation')
 e=np.r_[10000.,equity];dd=e/np.maximum.accumulate(e)-1
 pnls=np.array([t['net_pnl'] for t in trades]);wins=pnls[pnls>0];losses=pnls[pnls<0]
 de=np.r_[10000.,list(daily.values())];dr=de[1:]/de[:-1]-1
 metrics={'return_pct':(cash/10000-1)*100,'max_drawdown_pct':-dd.min()*100,'trades':len(trades),'win_rate_pct':100*np.mean(pnls>0) if len(pnls) else None,'profit_factor':wins.sum()/-losses.sum() if len(losses) else None,'mean_net_trade_usdt':pnls.mean() if len(pnls) else None,'daily_sharpe_365':np.mean(dr)/np.std(dr,ddof=1)*np.sqrt(365) if len(dr)>1 and np.std(dr,ddof=1)>0 else None,'costs_usdt':cost_total,'exposure_fraction':exposed/len(locs),'final_equity':cash}
 return metrics,trades,pd.DataFrame({'date':list(daily),'equity':list(daily.values())})

def self_test():
 # Synthetic ENGINE checks only; these prices are never used as historical results.
 idx=pd.date_range('2025-01-01',periods=864,freq='5min',tz='UTC');p=100+np.sin(np.arange(864)/11)+np.arange(864)*.02
 df=pd.DataFrame({'open':p,'high':p+.4,'low':p-.4,'close':p+.1,'volume':100000,'quote_volume':10000000,'trades':1000},index=idx)
 full=features(df);part=features(df.iloc[:401]);pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(full.iloc[:401],part)
 # Changing future observations must not alter past features.
 alt=df.copy();alt.iloc[401:,alt.columns.get_loc('close')]*=1.1
 pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(full.iloc[:401],features(alt).iloc[:401])
 for s in STRATEGIES:
  zero,t,_=run_one(full,s,0,0,'2025-01-01','2025-01-04')
  paid,pt,_=run_one(full,s,10,2,'2025-01-01','2025-01-04')
  for tr in pt:
   assert pd.Timestamp(tr['exit_time']).date()==pd.Timestamp(tr['entry_time']).date()
   assert 0<tr['bars_held']<=12
  assert abs(sum(x['net_pnl'] for x in pt)-(paid['final_equity']-10000))<1e-7
 # Exact zero-price-move fee accounting with forced EMA entries.
 flat=df.copy();flat[['open','high','low','close']]=100.;ff=features(flat);ff['fast']=99.;ff['slow']=98.;ff['atr']=1.
 z,_,_=run_one(ff,STRATEGIES[0],0,0,'2025-01-01','2025-01-04');cost,_,_=run_one(ff,STRATEGIES[0],10,2,'2025-01-01','2025-01-04')
 assert abs(z['return_pct'])<1e-10 and cost['return_pct']<0
 print('PASS engine checks: causal features, future perturbation, same-day exits, holding limits, ledger reconciliation, and fee drag.',flush=True)

def run():
 out=ROOT/'results';out.mkdir(exist_ok=True);(ROOT/'config.json').write_text(json.dumps(CONFIG,indent=2))
 rows=[];quality=[];alltrades=[];alldaily=[]
 for symbol in SYMBOLS:
  raw=load_data(symbol);quality.append({'symbol':symbol,'bars_5m':len(raw),'first':str(raw.index[0]),'last':str(raw.index[-1]),'duplicates':0,'missing_bars':0,'zero_volume_bars':int((raw.volume==0).sum())})
  for minutes in [5,15]:
   f=features(bars(raw,minutes))
   for strategy in STRATEGIES:
    for scenario,(fee,slip) in COSTS.items():
     for period,(start,end) in PERIODS.items():
      metrics,trades,daily=run_one(f,strategy,fee,slip,start,end)
      tags={'symbol':symbol,'interval_minutes':minutes,'strategy':strategy,'scenario':scenario,'fee_bps':fee,'slippage_bps':slip,'period':period}
      rows.append({**tags,**metrics})
      if scenario=='base_assumption' and period=='2025_full':
       alltrades.extend({**tags,**t} for t in trades);alldaily.append(daily.assign(**tags))
  print('Finished',symbol,flush=True)
 pd.DataFrame(rows).to_csv(out/'metrics.csv',index=False)
 pd.DataFrame(alltrades).to_csv(out/'base_trades.csv',index=False)
 pd.concat(alldaily,ignore_index=True).to_csv(out/'base_daily_equity.csv',index=False)
 (out/'data_quality.json').write_text(json.dumps(quality,indent=2))
 meta={'run_at_utc':dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).isoformat(),'python':sys.version,'numpy':np.__version__,'pandas':pd.__version__,'code_sha256':hashlib.sha256(Path(__file__).read_bytes()).hexdigest(),'metric_rows':len(rows),'status':'research_only','optimization':'none; parameters fixed before viewing results','not_true_prospective_holdout':'2025_H2 is a chronological diagnostic, not an untouched future trial; no claim of independent validation'}
 (out/'run_metadata.json').write_text(json.dumps(meta,indent=2));print(meta,flush=True)

if __name__=='__main__':
 p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('--download',action='store_true');p.add_argument('--run',action='store_true');p.add_argument('--self-test',action='store_true');a=p.parse_args()
 if a.self_test:self_test()
 if a.download:download()
 if a.run:run()
```

Trading_Research_Batch_02/research_improvements.py

```python
"""Bounded, predeclared personal research. No broker capability.
Uses Batch 01's checksum-verified 2025 history for development.
Unseen 2026 data may be opened only after development selection is frozen.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import sys,json,hashlib,datetime as dt,argparse,zipfile,urllib.request,concurrent.futures
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT.parent/'Trading_Research_Batch_01'))
import research_backtest as first
SYMBOLS=first.SYMBOLS
MODELS=['B02_EMA_CONTROL','B02_EMA_FILTERED','B02_CHANNEL_CONTROL','B02_CHANNEL_FILTERED','B02_VWAP_CONTROL','B02_VWAP_FILTERED']
SCENARIOS={'frictionless':(0,0,0),'base':(10,2,0),'stress':(10,10,0),'delayed':(10,2,1)}
CONFIG={'experiment':'Batch 02','symbols':SYMBOLS,'models':MODELS,'interval_minutes':15,'development':'2025-01-01 through 2025-12-31','validation_if_selected':'2026-01-01 through 2026-09-30','scenarios_fee_slippage_bps_extra_latency_bars':SCENARIOS,'allocation':.10,'participation_prior_quote_volume':.001,'min_notional':10,'max_entries_day':2,'cooldown_bars':4,'max_hold_bars':16,'stop_close_atr':2,'target_close_atr':4,'entry_utc':'02:00 through 19:30','flatten_utc':'23:45','hourly_regime':'last completed 1h close > EMA50 and EMA50 > value 3 completed hours earlier','selection':'family must have median positive net return and at least 4 of 6 positive assets in full 2025; positive median H1 and H2; positive median stress and delayed full-year returns; each asset at least 50 trades; each asset max drawdown <= 10%; select at most one family by median full-year base return; do not select instruments','qualification':'same criteria on full 2026 validation plus positive median return in each of 3 calendar quarters; no parameter changes after development; candidate still research-only','trial_history':'48 base configurations in prior batch; 36 fixed base configurations here; no grid search','rationale':'reduce churn, use fresh signals and compare regime/liquidity filters in three matched pairs; no profitability assumption'}

def prepare(raw):
 f=first.features(first.bars(raw,15));c=f.close;prev=c.shift(1)
 # Discard partial hours; observations become available only when the hour ends.
 hg=f.resample('1h',label='left',closed='left');h=hg.agg({'close':'last'})
 h=h[hg.size()==4].copy();h['hour_ema']=h.close.ewm(span=50,adjust=False,min_periods=50).mean();h['regime']=(h.close>h.hour_ema)&(h.hour_ema>h.hour_ema.shift(3));h.index=h.index+pd.Timedelta(hours=1)
 known=pd.DataFrame(index=f.index+pd.Timedelta(minutes=15)).join(h[['regime']],how='left').ffill();known.index=f.index
 # Exact hourly boundaries are allowed. Forward fill only known complete hours.
 f['regime']=known.regime.fillna(False).astype(bool)
 f['volume_reference']=f.quote_volume.shift(1).rolling(20).median()
 f['room']=(2*f.atr/f.close)>=3*.0024
 ema=(f.fast>f.slow)&(c>f.fast)&(prev<=f.fast.shift(1))
 threshold=f.upper+.1*f.atr
 channel=(c>threshold)&(prev<=threshold.shift(1))
 vwap=(c<f.vwap-2*f.price_std)&(c>prev)
 f['B02_EMA_CONTROL']=ema
 f['B02_EMA_FILTERED']=ema&f.regime&f.room
 f['B02_CHANNEL_CONTROL']=channel
 f['B02_CHANNEL_FILTERED']=channel&f.regime&f.room&(f.quote_volume>1.5*f.volume_reference)
 f['B02_VWAP_CONTROL']=vwap
 f['B02_VWAP_FILTERED']=vwap&f.regime&((2*f.price_std/f.close)>=3*.0024)
 return f

def simulate(f,model,scenario,start,end,retain=False):
 fee_bp,slip_bp,delay=SCENARIOS[scenario];fee=fee_bp/10000;slip=slip_bp/10000
 idx=f.index;n=len(f);mins=idx.hour.to_numpy()*60+idx.minute.to_numpy();days=idx.strftime('%Y-%m-%d').to_numpy()
 openp=f.open.to_numpy();close=f.close.to_numpy();atr=f.atr.to_numpy();qv=f.quote_volume.to_numpy();signal=f[model].fillna(False).to_numpy()
 fast=f.fast.to_numpy();slow=f.slow.to_numpy();lower=f.lower.to_numpy();vwap=f.vwap.to_numpy()
 locs=np.flatnonzero((idx>=pd.Timestamp(start,tz='UTC'))&(idx<pd.Timestamp(end,tz='UTC')))
 cash=10000.;qty=0.;stop=target=0.;entry_i=-1;entry_cost=entry_price=0.;last_exit=-100;entry_day=None;entries_today=0;eq=[];ledger=[];daily={};costs=0.;bars_exposed=0
 for i in locs:
  j=i-1-delay;exited=False
  if entry_day!=days[i]:entry_day=days[i];entries_today=0
  if qty:
   sj=i-1-delay
   signal_exit=(close[sj]<slow[sj]) if 'EMA' in model else (close[sj]<lower[sj]) if 'CHANNEL' in model else (close[sj]>=vwap[sj])
   reason='session' if mins[i]>=1425 else 'max_hold' if i-entry_i>=16 else 'close_stop' if close[sj]<=stop else 'close_target' if close[sj]>=target else 'signal' if signal_exit else None
   if reason:
    price=openp[i]*(1-slip);proceeds=qty*price*(1-fee);costs+=qty*price*fee+qty*openp[i]*slip;cash+=proceeds
    ledger.append({'entry_time':str(idx[entry_i]),'exit_time':str(idx[i]),'entry_price':entry_price,'exit_price':price,'quantity':qty,'entry_cost':entry_cost,'net_pnl':proceeds-entry_cost,'return_on_entry_cost':(proceeds-entry_cost)/entry_cost,'bars_held':i-entry_i,'reason':reason});qty=0.;exited=True;last_exit=i
  if not qty and not exited and i>=220 and j>=0 and 120<=mins[i]<=1170 and days[j]==days[i] and entries_today<2 and i-last_exit>=4 and signal[j] and np.isfinite(atr[j]) and atr[j]>0:
   budget=min(.1*cash,.001*qv[j]);entry_price=openp[i]*(1+slip)
   if budget>=10:
    qty=budget/(entry_price*(1+fee));entry_cost=budget;cash-=budget;entry_i=i;stop=entry_price-2*atr[j];target=entry_price+4*atr[j];entries_today+=1;costs+=qty*entry_price*fee+qty*openp[i]*slip
  bars_exposed+=int(qty>0);eq.append(cash+qty*close[i]);daily[days[i]]=eq[-1]
 if qty:raise AssertionError('Unclosed position at end; no invented exit')
 e=np.r_[10000.,eq];dd=1-e/np.maximum.accumulate(e);pnl=np.array([t['net_pnl'] for t in ledger]);wins=pnl[pnl>0];losses=pnl[pnl<0]
 d=np.r_[10000.,list(daily.values())];dr=d[1:]/d[:-1]-1
 metrics={'return_pct':(cash/10000-1)*100,'max_drawdown_pct':dd.max()*100,'trades':len(ledger),'costs_usdt':costs,'win_rate_pct':100*np.mean(pnl>0) if len(pnl) else None,'mean_net_trade_bps':np.mean([t['return_on_entry_cost'] for t in ledger])*10000 if len(pnl) else None,'profit_factor':wins.sum()/-losses.sum() if len(losses) else None,'daily_sharpe_365':dr.mean()/dr.std(ddof=1)*np.sqrt(365) if len(dr)>1 and dr.std(ddof=1)>0 else None,'final_equity':cash,'exposure_fraction':bars_exposed/len(locs)}
 assert abs(sum(t['net_pnl'] for t in ledger)-(cash-10000))<1e-6
 for t in ledger:
  assert pd.Timestamp(t['entry_time']).date()==pd.Timestamp(t['exit_time']).date() and 0<t['bars_held']<=16
 return metrics,ledger,pd.DataFrame({'date':list(daily),'equity':list(daily.values())})

def freeze():
 path=ROOT/'preregistration.json';payload={'registered_at_utc':dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).isoformat(),'config':CONFIG,'code_sha256':hashlib.sha256(Path(__file__).read_bytes()).hexdigest(),'2026_opened':False}
 if path.exists():
  old=json.loads(path.read_text());assert old['code_sha256']==payload['code_sha256'] and old['config']==json.loads(json.dumps(CONFIG)),'Frozen code/config changed; register a separate experiment instead.'
 else:path.write_text(json.dumps(payload,indent=2))
 print('Registered fixed rules before Batch 02 results; code hash',payload['code_sha256'],flush=True)

def check_engine():
 idx=pd.date_range('2025-01-01',periods=1728,freq='5min',tz='UTC');p=100+np.sin(np.arange(len(idx))/17)+np.arange(len(idx))*.003
 raw=pd.DataFrame({'open':p,'high':p+.5,'low':p-.5,'close':p+.1,'volume':100000,'quote_volume':10000000,'trades':100},index=idx)
 a=prepare(raw);b=prepare(raw.iloc[:900]);pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(a.iloc[:len(b)],b)
 changed=raw.copy();changed.iloc[900:,changed.columns.get_loc('close')]*=1.02;pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(a.iloc[:len(b)],prepare(changed).iloc[:len(b)])
 for model in MODELS:
  for sc in ['base','delayed']:simulate(a,model,sc,'2025-01-01','2025-01-07')
 flat=raw.copy();flat[['open','high','low','close']]=100.;f=prepare(flat);f[MODELS[0]]=True;f['atr']=1.;f['slow']=99.
 z,_,_=simulate(f,MODELS[0],'frictionless','2025-01-01','2025-01-07');paid,t,_=simulate(f,MODELS[0],'base','2025-01-01','2025-01-07');assert abs(z['return_pct'])<1e-10 and paid['return_pct']<0
 grouped=pd.DataFrame(t).assign(day=lambda x:x.entry_time.str[:10]).groupby('day').size();assert (grouped<=2).all()
 for earlier,later in zip(t,t[1:]):assert (pd.Timestamp(later['entry_time'])-pd.Timestamp(earlier['exit_time'])).total_seconds()>=4*900
 print('PASS causal completed-hour alignment, future perturbation, accounting, costs, entry limits, cooldown, same-day exits and maximum holding checks.',flush=True)

def run_period(raw,phase,periods,models):
 out=ROOT/'results';out.mkdir(exist_ok=True);allrows=[];ledgers=[];daily=[]
 for symbol in SYMBOLS:
  f=prepare(raw(symbol))
  for model in models:
   for sc in SCENARIOS:
    for period,(start,end) in periods.items():
     result,tr,e=simulate(f,model,sc,start,end);tags={'symbol':symbol,'model':model,'scenario':sc,'period':period,'phase':phase};allrows.append({**tags,**result})
     if sc=='base' and period=='full':ledgers.extend({**tags,**t} for t in tr);daily.append(e.assign(**tags))
  print(phase,'finished',symbol,flush=True)
 pd.DataFrame(allrows).to_csv(out/f'{phase}_metrics.csv',index=False)
 pd.DataFrame(ledgers).to_csv(out/f'{phase}_trades.csv',index=False);pd.concat(daily,ignore_index=True).to_csv(out/f'{phase}_daily.csv',index=False)
 return pd.DataFrame(allrows)

def select(m):
 rows=[]
 for model in MODELS:
  x=m[m.model==model];b=x[(x.scenario=='base')&(x.period=='full')];h1=x[(x.scenario=='base')&(x.period=='H1')];h2=x[(x.scenario=='base')&(x.period=='H2')];stress=x[(x.scenario=='stress')&(x.period=='full')];delay=x[(x.scenario=='delayed')&(x.period=='full')]
  gates={'median_base_positive':bool(b.return_pct.median()>0),'at_least_four_assets_positive':bool((b.return_pct>0).sum()>=4),'both_halves_median_positive':bool(h1.return_pct.median()>0 and h2.return_pct.median()>0),'stress_median_positive':bool(stress.return_pct.median()>0),'delayed_median_positive':bool(delay.return_pct.median()>0),'each_asset_50_trades':bool((b.trades>=50).all()),'each_asset_drawdown_below_10pct':bool((b.max_drawdown_pct<=10).all())}
  rows.append({'model':model,'median_base_return_pct':float(b.return_pct.median()),'positive_assets':int((b.return_pct>0).sum()),'minimum_trades':int(b.trades.min()),'gates':gates,'eligible':all(gates.values())})
 passed=[r for r in rows if r['eligible']];chosen=max(passed,key=lambda r:r['median_base_return_pct'])['model'] if passed else None
 result={'selected_at_utc':dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).isoformat(),'models':rows,'selected_model':chosen,'2026_data_opened_before_selection':False,'qualifying':False}
 (ROOT/'selection.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2));print(json.dumps(result,indent=2),flush=True)
 return result

def download_2026():
 (ROOT/'data').mkdir(exist_ok=True)
 def one(item):
  s,month=item;name=f'{s}-5m-2026-{month:02d}.zip';url=f'https://data.binance.vision/data/spot/monthly/klines/{s}/5m/{name}';p=ROOT/'data'/name;cp=p.with_suffix('.zip.CHECKSUM')
  if not p.exists():p.write_bytes(urllib.request.urlopen(url,timeout=40).read())
  if not cp.exists():cp.write_bytes(urllib.request.urlopen(url+'.CHECKSUM',timeout=40).read())
  actual=hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest();assert actual==cp.read_text().split()[0]
  return {'file':name,'url':url,'sha256':actual,'bytes':p.stat().st_size}
 rows=[];errors=[]
 with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as pool:
  fs={pool.submit(one,(s,m)):(s,m) for s in SYMBOLS for m in range(1,10)}
  for f in concurrent.futures.as_completed(fs):
   try:rows.append(f.result())
   except Exception as e:errors.append({'item':fs[f],'error':str(e)})
 manifest={'retrieved_at_utc':dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).isoformat(),'archives':rows,'errors':errors};(ROOT/'validation_data_manifest.json').write_text(json.dumps(manifest,indent=2))
 if errors:raise RuntimeError('Validation data unavailable/incomplete; no substitution. See manifest.')

def load_2026(symbol):
 cols=['time','open','high','low','close','volume','close_time','quote_volume','trades','taker_base','taker_quote','ignore'];frames=[]
 for month in range(1,10):
  p=ROOT/'data'/f'{symbol}-5m-2026-{month:02d}.zip';assert hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()==p.with_suffix('.zip.CHECKSUM').read_text().split()[0]
  with zipfile.ZipFile(p) as z:
   names=[n for n in z.namelist() if n.endswith('.csv')];assert len(names)==1;frames.append(pd.read_csv(z.open(names[0]),header=None,names=cols))
 f=pd.concat(frames,ignore_index=True);f.time=pd.to_datetime(f.time,unit='us',utc=True);assert not f.time.duplicated().any();f=f.set_index('time').sort_index()
 expected=pd.date_range('2026-01-01','2026-10-01',freq='5min',inclusive='left',tz='UTC');assert f.index.equals(expected)
 assert np.isfinite(f[['open','high','low','close','volume','quote_volume']]).all().all();assert (f[['open','high','low','close']]>0).all().all();assert (f[['volume','quote_volume']]>=0).all().all()
 assert (f.high>=f[['open','close','low']].max(axis=1)).all() and (f.low<=f[['open','close','high']].min(axis=1)).all()
 return pd.concat([first.load_data(symbol).iloc[-1200:],f])

def validate(selected):
 if not selected['selected_model']:
  (ROOT/'validation_status.json').write_text(json.dumps({'status':'NOT_OPENED','reason':'No model passed development gates. 2026 validation remains unused.','qualifying':False},indent=2));return
 try:
  download_2026();m=run_period(load_2026,'validation',{'full':('2026-01-01','2026-10-01'),'Q1':('2026-01-01','2026-04-01'),'Q2':('2026-04-01','2026-07-01'),'Q3':('2026-07-01','2026-10-01')},[selected['selected_model']])
  b=m[(m.scenario=='base')&(m.period=='full')];stress=m[(m.scenario=='stress')&(m.period=='full')];delay=m[(m.scenario=='delayed')&(m.period=='full')]
  gates={'median_positive':bool(b.return_pct.median()>0),'four_positive_assets':bool((b.return_pct>0).sum()>=4),'minimum_50_trades':bool((b.trades>=50).all()),'max_drawdown_below_10pct':bool((b.max_drawdown_pct<=10).all()),'stress_positive':bool(stress.return_pct.median()>0),'delayed_positive':bool(delay.return_pct.median()>0),'every_quarter_median_positive':all(m[(m.scenario=='base')&(m.period==q)].return_pct.median()>0 for q in ['Q1','Q2','Q3'])}
  result={'status':'TEST_COMPLETE','gates':gates,'qualifying':bool(all(gates.values())),'scope':'historical research qualification only; no live eligibility; not prospective; dependent candidate trials not formally selection-adjusted'}
 except Exception as e:result={'status':'DATA_BLOCKED','error':str(e),'qualifying':False}
 (ROOT/'validation_status.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2));print(result,flush=True)

if __name__=='__main__':
 p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('--register',action='store_true');p.add_argument('--self-test',action='store_true');p.add_argument('--develop',action='store_true');p.add_argument('--validate',action='store_true');a=p.parse_args()
 if a.register:freeze()
 if a.self_test:check_engine()
 if a.develop:
  freeze();m=run_period(first.load_data,'development',{'full':('2025-01-01','2026-01-01'),'H1':('2025-01-01','2025-07-01'),'H2':('2025-07-01','2026-01-01')},MODELS);selection=select(m)
 if a.validate:validate(json.loads((ROOT/'selection.json').read_text()))
```

Trading_Research_Batch_03/research_long_short.py

```python
"""Personal non-production long/short futures research; no trading connection."""
from pathlib import Path
import sys,json,hashlib,datetime as dt,zipfile,urllib.request,concurrent.futures,argparse
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0,str(ROOT.parent/'Trading_Research_Batch_01'))
import research_backtest as first
SYMBOLS=['BTCUSDT','ETHUSDT','SOLUSDT']
MODELS=['B03_EMA_LONG_SHORT','B03_CHANNEL_LONG_SHORT']
SCENARIOS={'frictionless':(0,0,0),'base':(10,2,0),'stress':(10,10,0),'delayed':(10,2,1)}
CONFIG={'symbols':SYMBOLS,'models':MODELS,'timeframe':'15m','market':'Binance USD-M perpetual futures, actual futures and mark-price bars plus funding events','development':'2025 full year and half-year diagnostics','validation_if_selected':'2026 January-September and quarter diagnostics','allocation_fraction':.1,'quote_volume_participation_cap':.001,'min_notional':10,'max_entries_day':2,'cooldown_bars':4,'max_hold_bars':16,'entry_window_utc':'02:00-19:30','flatten_utc':'23:45','stop_close_ATR14':2,'target_close_ATR14':4,'regime':'last completed hourly close above/below EMA50 with matching EMA slope over 3 completed hours','range_screen':'2 ATR >= 3 times assumed base roundtrip cost of 24 bps','EMA_signal':'20/50 trend plus fresh cross of close through EMA20 in trend direction','channel_signal':'fresh break beyond prior 20-bar high/low plus 0.1 ATR, quote volume greater than 1.5 times prior 20-bar median','funding':'actual timestamped rates; last known 15m mark open is funding-price approximation; apply only if position held across event; no funding prediction signal','fee_slippage_latency':SCENARIOS,'selection':'median positive base/stress/delayed return; >=2/3 positive instruments; positive median H1/H2; >=50 trades each; max DD <=10% each; select at most one fixed model by median base return; no asset selection','qualification':'same full validation criteria plus each validation quarter median positive; historical research only, never live readiness','trial_count':'48 Batch01 +36 Batch02 +6 Batch03 base configurations; no grid search','limits':'no lot rounding, measured fills, tiered maintenance margin, liquidation engine, collateral interest, or ADL model; coarse participation cap; no account/provider recommendation'}

def register():
 p=ROOT/'preregistration.json';payload={'registered_at_utc':dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).isoformat(),'config':CONFIG,'code_sha256':hashlib.sha256(Path(__file__).read_bytes()).hexdigest(),'2026_opened':False}
 if p.exists():
  old=json.loads(p.read_text());assert old['code_sha256']==payload['code_sha256'] and old['config']==json.loads(json.dumps(CONFIG))
 else:p.write_text(json.dumps(payload,indent=2))
 print('Batch 03 frozen before its performance calculation:',payload['code_sha256'],flush=True)

def download(year,months):
 data=ROOT/'data';data.mkdir(exist_ok=True);jobs=[(s,m,kind) for s in SYMBOLS for m in months for kind in ['klines','markPriceKlines','fundingRate']]
 def one(job):
  s,m,kind=job;interval=None if kind=='fundingRate' else '15m';name=s+'-'+('fundingRate' if interval is None else interval)+f'-{year}-{m:02d}.zip';url=f'https://data.binance.vision/data/futures/um/monthly/{kind}/{s}/'+('' if interval is None else interval+'/')+name;p=data/(kind+'_'+name);cp=p.with_suffix('.zip.CHECKSUM')
  if not p.exists():p.write_bytes(urllib.request.urlopen(url,timeout=40).read())
  if not cp.exists():cp.write_bytes(urllib.request.urlopen(url+'.CHECKSUM',timeout=40).read())
  h=hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest();assert h==cp.read_text().split()[0]
  return {'symbol':s,'month':f'{year}-{m:02d}','kind':kind,'file':p.name,'url':url,'sha256':h,'bytes':p.stat().st_size}
 rows=[];errors=[]
 with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as pool:
  fs={pool.submit(one,job):job for job in jobs}
  for f in concurrent.futures.as_completed(fs):
   try:rows.append(f.result())
   except Exception as e:errors.append({'job':fs[f],'error':str(e)})
   if (len(rows)+len(errors))%18==0:print('Archives checked',len(rows),'errors',len(errors),flush=True)
 out={'retrieved_at_utc':dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).isoformat(),'archives':sorted(rows,key=lambda r:r['file']),'errors':errors,'terms':'https://github.com/binance/binance-public-data/blob/master/TERMS_AND_CONDITIONS.md','scope':'personal non-production research'};(ROOT/f'data_manifest_{year}.json').write_text(json.dumps(out,indent=2))
 if errors:raise RuntimeError('Incomplete archives; no substitution')

def load(symbol,year,months):
 datasets={}
 for kind in ['klines','markPriceKlines','fundingRate']:
  frames=[]
  for m in months:
   suffix='fundingRate' if kind=='fundingRate' else '15m';p=ROOT/'data'/f'{kind}_{symbol}-{suffix}-{year}-{m:02d}.zip'
   assert hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()==p.with_suffix('.zip.CHECKSUM').read_text().split()[0]
   with zipfile.ZipFile(p) as z:
    names=[n for n in z.namelist() if n.endswith('.csv')];assert len(names)==1;frames.append(pd.read_csv(z.open(names[0])))
  f=pd.concat(frames,ignore_index=True)
  timecol='calc_time' if kind=='fundingRate' else 'open_time';f[timecol]=pd.to_datetime(f[timecol],unit='ms',utc=True);assert not f[timecol].duplicated().any();f=f.set_index(timecol).sort_index()
  if kind!='fundingRate':
   end=pd.Timestamp(year=year,month=months[-1],day=1,tz='UTC')+pd.offsets.MonthBegin(1);expected=pd.date_range(pd.Timestamp(year=year,month=months[0],day=1,tz='UTC'),end,freq='15min',inclusive='left');assert f.index.equals(expected)
   assert np.isfinite(f[['open','high','low','close','volume','quote_volume']]).all().all();assert (f[['open','high','low','close']]>0).all().all();assert (f[['volume','quote_volume']]>=0).all().all();assert (f.high>=f[['open','close','low']].max(axis=1)).all() and (f.low<=f[['open','close','high']].min(axis=1)).all()
  else:
   assert np.isfinite(f[['funding_interval_hours','last_funding_rate']]).all().all() and (f.funding_interval_hours>0).all()
   gap=f.index.to_series().diff().dt.total_seconds()/3600;allowed=pd.concat([f.funding_interval_hours,f.funding_interval_hours.shift(1)],axis=1).max(axis=1)
   assert (gap.iloc[1:]<=allowed.iloc[1:]+.01).all(),'Funding gap exceeds reported intervals'
   assert f.index[0].floor('D')==pd.Timestamp(year=year,month=months[0],day=1,tz='UTC')
   assert f.index[-1].ceil('D')>=pd.Timestamp(year=year,month=months[-1],day=1,tz='UTC')+pd.offsets.MonthBegin(1)
  datasets[kind]=f
 assert datasets['klines'].index.equals(datasets['markPriceKlines'].index)
 return datasets['klines'],datasets['markPriceKlines'],datasets['fundingRate']

def features(f):
 x=first.features(f);hg=x.resample('1h');h=hg.agg({'close':'last'});h=h[hg.size()==4].copy();h['ema']=h.close.ewm(span=50,adjust=False,min_periods=50).mean();h['up']=(h.close>h.ema)&(h.ema>h.ema.shift(3));h['down']=(h.close<h.ema)&(h.ema<h.ema.shift(3));h.index+=pd.Timedelta(hours=1)
 known=pd.DataFrame(index=x.index+pd.Timedelta(minutes=15)).join(h[['up','down']]).ffill();known.index=x.index;up=known.up.eq(True);down=known.down.eq(True);room=2*x.atr/x.close>=3*.0024
 c=x.close;prev=c.shift(1);longema=(x.fast>x.slow)&(c>x.fast)&(prev<=x.fast.shift(1));shortema=(x.fast<x.slow)&(c<x.fast)&(prev>=x.fast.shift(1));volume=x.quote_volume>1.5*x.quote_volume.shift(1).rolling(20).median()
 lo=x.low.shift(1).rolling(20).min();hi=x.high.shift(1).rolling(20).max();lowbar=lo-.1*x.atr;highbar=hi+.1*x.atr
 lc=(c>highbar)&(prev<=highbar.shift(1));sc=(c<lowbar)&(prev>=lowbar.shift(1))
 x[MODELS[0]]=np.select([longema&up&room,shortema&down&room],[1,-1],default=0)
 x[MODELS[1]]=np.select([lc&up&room&volume,sc&down&room&volume],[1,-1],default=0)
 x['exit_upper']=x.high.shift(1).rolling(10).max();return x

def simulate(f,mark,funding,model,scenario,start,end):
 fee_bp,slip_bp,delay=SCENARIOS[scenario];fee=fee_bp/10000;slip=slip_bp/10000;idx=f.index
 o=f.open.to_numpy();c=f.close.to_numpy();mc=mark.close.to_numpy();atr=f.atr.to_numpy();qv=f.quote_volume.to_numpy();sig=f[model].to_numpy();slow=f.slow.to_numpy();lo=f.lower.to_numpy();hi=f.exit_upper.to_numpy();mins=idx.hour.to_numpy()*60+idx.minute.to_numpy();day=idx.strftime('%Y-%m-%d').to_numpy()
 # A funding event is applied at the next observation open after its exact timestamp.
 # Since positions have no intrabar fills, this captures the owner across the event.
 events={}
 for timestamp,r in funding.iterrows():
  i=idx.searchsorted(timestamp,side='left');k=mark.index.searchsorted(timestamp,side='right')-1
  if i<len(idx) and k>=0:events.setdefault(i,[]).append((timestamp,float(r.last_funding_rate),float(mark.open.iloc[k])))
 locs=np.flatnonzero((idx>=pd.Timestamp(start,tz='UTC'))&(idx<pd.Timestamp(end,tz='UTC')))
 cash=10000.;qty=0.;side=0;entry_i=-1;entry_price=entry_fee=entry_funding=0.;last_exit=-100;entries=0;entryday=None;eq=[];trades=[];daily={};fee_slip=0.;fund_total=0.;exposed=0;minimum_margin_ratio=float('inf')
 for i in locs:
  j=i-1-delay;exited=False
  if entryday!=day[i]:entryday=day[i];entries=0
  if qty:
   for timestamp,rate,price in events.get(i,[]):
    if timestamp>idx[entry_i]:
     change=-side*qty*price*rate;cash+=change;entry_funding+=change;fund_total+=change
   exit_signal=(side*(c[j]-slow[j])<0) if 'EMA' in model else (c[j]<lo[j] if side==1 else c[j]>hi[j])
   pnl_distance=side*(c[j]-entry_price)
   reason='session' if mins[i]>=1425 else 'max_hold' if i-entry_i>=16 else 'close_stop' if pnl_distance<=-2*entry_atr else 'close_target' if pnl_distance>=4*entry_atr else 'signal' if exit_signal else None
   if reason:
    price=o[i]*(1-side*slip);exitfee=qty*price*fee;profit=side*qty*(price-entry_price)-exitfee;cash+=profit;fee_slip+=exitfee+qty*o[i]*slip
    trades.append({'entry_time':str(idx[entry_i]),'exit_time':str(idx[i]),'side':side,'quantity':qty,'entry_price':entry_price,'exit_price':price,'net_pnl':profit-entry_fee+entry_funding,'funding_pnl':entry_funding,'bars_held':i-entry_i,'reason':reason});qty=0.;side=0;last_exit=i;exited=True
  if not qty and not exited and i>=220 and j>=0 and 120<=mins[i]<=1170 and day[j]==day[i] and entries<2 and i-last_exit>=4 and sig[j] and np.isfinite(atr[j]) and atr[j]>0:
   budget=min(.1*cash,.001*qv[j]);side=int(sig[j]);entry_price=o[i]*(1+side*slip)
   if budget>=10:
    qty=budget/(entry_price*(1+fee));entry_fee=qty*entry_price*fee;cash-=entry_fee;entry_i=i;entry_atr=atr[j];entry_funding=0.;entries+=1;fee_slip+=entry_fee+qty*o[i]*slip
   else:side=0
  value=cash+side*qty*(mc[i]-entry_price) if qty else cash
  if qty:
   # Intrabar adverse mark extreme is diagnostic only, not an invented fill.
   worst=mark.low.iloc[i] if side==1 else mark.high.iloc[i];worst_equity=cash+side*qty*(worst-entry_price);assert worst_equity>0,'Collateral exhausted; no liquidation simulation supplied'
   minimum_margin_ratio=min(minimum_margin_ratio,worst_equity/(qty*worst))
  exposed+=int(qty>0);eq.append(value);daily[day[i]]=value
 assert not qty
 assert abs(sum(t['net_pnl'] for t in trades)-(cash-10000))<1e-6
 for t in trades:assert pd.Timestamp(t['entry_time']).date()==pd.Timestamp(t['exit_time']).date() and 0<t['bars_held']<=16
 e=np.r_[10000.,eq];pnl=np.array([t['net_pnl'] for t in trades]);metrics={'return_pct':(cash/10000-1)*100,'max_drawdown_pct':100*(1-e/np.maximum.accumulate(e)).max(),'trades':len(trades),'long_trades':sum(t['side']==1 for t in trades),'short_trades':sum(t['side']==-1 for t in trades),'win_rate_pct':100*np.mean(pnl>0) if len(pnl) else None,'fee_slippage_usdt':fee_slip,'funding_pnl_usdt':fund_total,'final_equity':cash,'minimum_collateral_to_notional_ratio':minimum_margin_ratio if np.isfinite(minimum_margin_ratio) else None,'exposure_fraction':exposed/len(locs)}
 return metrics,trades,pd.DataFrame({'date':list(daily),'equity':list(daily.values())})

def check_engine():
 idx=pd.date_range('2025-01-01',periods=576,freq='15min',tz='UTC');p=100+np.sin(np.arange(len(idx))/13)+np.arange(len(idx))*.001;raw=pd.DataFrame({'open':p,'high':p+.4,'low':p-.4,'close':p+.1,'volume':1e6,'quote_volume':1e8},index=idx);a=features(raw);b=features(raw.iloc[:299]);pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(a.iloc[:299],b)
 empty=pd.DataFrame(columns=['funding_interval_hours','last_funding_rate'],index=pd.DatetimeIndex([],tz='UTC'));flat=raw.copy();flat[['open','high','low','close']]=100.;f=features(flat);f.atr=1.
 fund=pd.DataFrame({'funding_interval_hours':8,'last_funding_rate':.001},index=pd.date_range('2025-01-01',periods=18,freq='8h',tz='UTC')+pd.Timedelta(milliseconds=15))
 for side in [1,-1]:
  f[MODELS[0]]=side;f.slow=99 if side==1 else 101
  zero,_,_=simulate(f,flat,empty,MODELS[0],'frictionless','2025-01-01','2025-01-07');paid,_,_=simulate(f,flat,empty,MODELS[0],'base','2025-01-01','2025-01-07');funded,tr,_=simulate(f,flat,fund,MODELS[0],'frictionless','2025-01-01','2025-01-07');assert abs(zero['return_pct'])<1e-10 and paid['return_pct']<0;assert side*funded['funding_pnl_usdt']<0
 print('PASS causal features, both-side fee drag, funding signs, same-day exits, holding limit, accounting and collateral checks.',flush=True)

def run(year,months,phase,periods,models):
 out=ROOT/'results';out.mkdir(exist_ok=True);rows=[];ledger=[];daily=[];quality=[]
 for s in SYMBOLS:
  raw,mark,fund=load(s,year,months);quality.append({'symbol':s,'bars':len(raw),'mark_bars':len(mark),'funding_events':len(fund),'missing_bars':0,'duplicate_times':0});f=features(raw)
  for model in models:
   for sc in SCENARIOS:
    for period,(start,end) in periods.items():
     metrics,tr,d=simulate(f,mark,fund,model,sc,start,end);tags={'symbol':s,'model':model,'scenario':sc,'period':period,'phase':phase};rows.append({**tags,**metrics})
     if sc=='base' and period=='full':ledger.extend({**tags,**t} for t in tr);daily.append(d.assign(**tags))
  print(phase,'finished',s,flush=True)
 m=pd.DataFrame(rows);m.to_csv(out/f'{phase}_metrics.csv',index=False);pd.DataFrame(ledger).to_csv(out/f'{phase}_trades.csv',index=False);pd.concat(daily,ignore_index=True).to_csv(out/f'{phase}_daily.csv',index=False);(out/f'{phase}_data_quality.json').write_text(json.dumps(quality,indent=2));return m

def choose(m):
 rows=[]
 for model in MODELS:
  x=m[m.model==model];b=x[(x.scenario=='base')&(x.period=='full')]
  gates={'base_median_positive':bool(b.return_pct.median()>0),'two_assets_positive':bool((b.return_pct>0).sum()>=2),'each_50_trades':bool((b.trades>=50).all()),'each_drawdown_below_10pct':bool((b.max_drawdown_pct<=10).all()),'stress_median_positive':bool(x[(x.scenario=='stress')&(x.period=='full')].return_pct.median()>0),'delayed_median_positive':bool(x[(x.scenario=='delayed')&(x.period=='full')].return_pct.median()>0),'each_half_median_positive':all(x[(x.scenario=='base')&(x.period==p)].return_pct.median()>0 for p in ['H1','H2'])}
  rows.append({'model':model,'median_base_return_pct':float(b.return_pct.median()),'gates':gates,'eligible':bool(all(gates.values()))})
 eligible=[r for r in rows if r['eligible']];selected=max(eligible,key=lambda r:r['median_base_return_pct'])['model'] if eligible else None
 result={'selected_at_utc':dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).isoformat(),'models':rows,'selected_model':selected,'2026_opened_before_selection':False};(ROOT/'selection.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2));print(json.dumps(result,indent=2),flush=True);return result

def validate(selected):
 if not selected['selected_model']:r={'status':'NOT_OPENED','qualifying':False,'reason':'No model passed development; 2026 validation unused.'}
 else:
  try:
   download(2026,list(range(1,10)));m=run(2026,list(range(1,10)),'validation',{'full':('2026-01-01','2026-10-01'),'Q1':('2026-01-01','2026-04-01'),'Q2':('2026-04-01','2026-07-01'),'Q3':('2026-07-01','2026-10-01')},[selected['selected_model']]);b=m[(m.scenario=='base')&(m.period=='full')]
   gates={'base_median_positive':bool(b.return_pct.median()>0),'two_assets_positive':bool((b.return_pct>0).sum()>=2),'each_50_trades':bool((b.trades>=50).all()),'each_drawdown_below_10pct':bool((b.max_drawdown_pct<=10).all()),'stress_positive':bool(m[(m.scenario=='stress')&(m.period=='full')].return_pct.median()>0),'delayed_positive':bool(m[(m.scenario=='delayed')&(m.period=='full')].return_pct.median()>0),'every_quarter_positive':all(m[(m.scenario=='base')&(m.period==p)].return_pct.median()>0 for p in ['Q1','Q2','Q3'])};r={'status':'TEST_COMPLETE','gates':gates,'qualifying':bool(all(gates.values())),'scope':'historical research only; funding-price approximation and incomplete liquidation model remain; no prospective/live validation'}
  except Exception as e:r={'status':'DATA_BLOCKED','error':str(e),'qualifying':False}
 (ROOT/'validation_status.json').write_text(json.dumps(r,indent=2));print(r,flush=True)

if __name__=='__main__':
 p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('--register',action='store_true');p.add_argument('--self-test',action='store_true');p.add_argument('--develop',action='store_true');p.add_argument('--validate',action='store_true');a=p.parse_args()
 if a.register:register()
 if a.self_test:check_engine()

 if a.validate:validate(json.loads((ROOT/'selection.json').read_text()))

```

Reproduction commands: create the three named folders, save the code blocks, and run the following in the parent folder. Batch 01 --download retrieves the frozen 2025 spot inputs used by Batch 02. Registration prevents code/config changes from silently replacing a completed experiment. A new hypothesis requires a separate version.

```bash
python Trading_Research_Batch_01/research_backtest.py --download --self-test --run
python Trading_Research_Batch_02/research_improvements.py --register --self-test --develop --validate
python Trading_Research_Batch_03/research_long_short.py --register --self-test --develop --validate
```

Only personal non-production historical research is included; no production data rights or live trading permissions are conferred.

Revision 13, October 10, 2026 (America/Edmonton): adds bounded spot-filter comparisons and long/short futures tests, frozen source and data provenance, explicit development/validation gates, and all outcomes. Exactly two documents remain the user-facing delivery. Historical research remains distinct from a functioning automated trading system.
